\usepackage[hidelinks]{hyperref}

\newcommand{\dictentry}[3]{%
  \par\addvspace{3pt}%
  \hangindent=0pt
  \hangafter=0
  \noindent{\color{#3}\textbf{#1}}\hspace{0.5em}{#2}\par
}

\newcommand{\booktitle}{Space Visual Dictionary}
\title{\Huge \booktitle}
\author{}

\begin{document}
% ===== AERODYNAMICS TERMS =====

% ===== AERODYNAMICS TERMS =====
\dictentry{Ablation}{Surface material removal by vaporization for thermal protection.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Ablative TPS}{A thermal protection system that dissipates heat through the controlled ablation (surface removal) of a sacrificial material, carrying heat away from the underlying structure. Ablative TPS is used for high-heat-flux applications such as ICBM re-entry vehicles and planetary probes.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Accelerometer}{A sensor measuring acceleration for vibration testing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Acoustic Testing}{A specialized wind tunnel testing methodology focused on measuring noise generated by aerodynamic flows. It requires anechoic or treated test sections with low background noise levels to accurately capture airframe, engine, and propeller noise signatures.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Adverse Yaw}{The tendency to yaw opposite to the roll direction due to differential drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aeroacoustics}{The branch of acoustics that studies noise generation by turbulent fluid motion or aerodynamic forces interacting with surfaces. It encompasses jet noise, airframe noise, propeller and rotor noise, and fan noise, and is critical for aircraft certification and community noise reduction.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aerodynamic Center}{The point on an airfoil about which the pitching moment is constant regardless of angle of attack.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aerodynamic Force}{The resultant force on a body moving through air, resolved into lift, drag, and side force.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aerodynamic Heating}{Frictional heating caused by the compression of air at high speeds.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aerodynamic Twist}{Variation of airfoil section angle along the span.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aerodynamics}{The study of how air flows around solid objects and the forces generated by moving air.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aeroelasticity}{The study of interaction between aerodynamic forces and structural deformation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aeroshell}{The protective outer shell of a spacecraft that provides aerodynamic deceleration and thermal protection during atmospheric entry. An aeroshell typically consists of a heat shield on the forward face and a backshell covering the aft side, enclosing the payload.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aerothermal Heating}{Heating of a body due to high-speed atmospheric passage.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Agility}{A measure of rapid attitude change capability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aileron}{A hinged control surface on the trailing edge of a wing that controls roll.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Airbrake}{A device that increases drag to decelerate an aircraft in flight.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Airfoil}{A cross-sectional shape designed to produce lift or control from the air.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Airframe Noise}{The aerodynamic noise produced by the airframe of an aircraft during flight, including noise from landing gear, flaps, slats, and wing trailing edges. As engines become quieter, airframe noise has become a dominant contributor to overall aircraft noise during approach and landing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Angle of Attack}{The angle between the chord line of an airfoil and the oncoming airflow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Angle of Incidence}{The angle between the wing chord line and the aircraft longitudinal axis.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Anhedral}{A downward angle of the wings decreasing lateral stability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Arc-Heated Wind Tunnel}{A tunnel using an electric arc to heat the test gas.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Area Rule}{A transonic aerodynamic design principle developed by Richard Whitcomb stating that the cross-sectional area of an aircraft should vary smoothly along its length to minimize wave drag at transonic speeds. The area rule led to the characteristic 'Coke bottle' fuselage shape of transonic aircraft and is critical for optimizing aerodynamic performance near Mach 1.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Aspect Ratio}{The ratio of wingspan to mean chord. Higher AR gives lower induced drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Atmospheric Re-entry}{The phase of a spacecraft's return trajectory from space through a planetary atmosphere, characterized by hypersonic velocities, intense aerodynamic heating, and high deceleration loads. Atmospheric re-entry requires thermal protection systems and careful trajectory design to ensure survival.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Attached Flow}{Airflow that smoothly follows the contour of a surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Attached Shock}{A shock attached to a sharp leading edge.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Autorotation}{The condition where a rotor turns due to airflow passing through it.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Balance}{A multi-component force measurement device.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Ballistic Re-entry}{A non-lifting re-entry trajectory in which the vehicle follows a purely gravitational path with no aerodynamic lift. Ballistic re-entry produces high peak deceleration and heating rates but is simpler and was used by early crewed capsules such as Mercury and Apollo.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Base Drag}{Drag from the low-pressure area at a blunt base.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Belly Flap}{A flap on the underside for drag or lift control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Bernoulli Equation}{Constant total pressure along a streamline in inviscid flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Blended Wing Body}{An aircraft configuration blending wing and fuselage.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Blockage}{The ratio of model frontal area to tunnel cross-section.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Boattail}{A tapered aft body contour to reduce base drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Boundary Layer}{The thin viscous region of flow adjacent to a surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Boundary Layer Control}{Methods to manipulate the state of the boundary layer.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Boundary Layer Separation}{Detachment of the boundary layer from the surface, causing a wake.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Boundary Layer Transition}{The change from laminar to turbulent flow in the boundary layer.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Bow Shock}{A detached shock wave ahead of a blunt body.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Buffeting}{Vibration from unsteady flow separation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Bump}{A protuberance on a surface for flow control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Buoyancy}{The upward force exerted by a fluid on a body immersed in it, equal to the weight of the fluid displaced by the body (Archimedes' principle). In aerodynamics, buoyancy is the principle behind lighter-than-air vehicles such as balloons and airships, and is proportional to the density difference between the lifting gas and the surrounding air.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Camber}{The curvature of the mean camber line of an airfoil.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Camber Line}{The line midway between the upper and lower airfoil surfaces.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Canard}{A small forewing ahead of the main wing for control or stability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Carbon Footprint}{The total amount of carbon dioxide (CO2) emissions attributable to an aircraft or aviation operation over a given period or mission. Aviation carbon footprint reduction strategies include sustainable aviation fuels, improved aerodynamics, lightweight structures, and optimized flight operations.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Catalytic Heating}{The additional heating that occurs when atomic species (oxygen and nitrogen) recombine on a spacecraft surface during re-entry, releasing chemical energy. Catalytic heating depends on the surface material's recombination efficiency and can significantly increase total heat flux.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Center of Pressure}{The point on an airfoil where the resultant aerodynamic force acts.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Chevron Nozzle}{A jet engine exhaust nozzle with a serrated or sawtooth trailing edge that promotes mixing between the hot exhaust jet and the ambient air. Chevrons reduce jet noise by breaking up large-scale turbulent structures and shifting the noise spectrum to higher frequencies.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Chine}{A sharp longitudinal edge on a fuselage for lift.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Choked Flow}{A duct condition where mass flow is limited by the throat reaching Mach 1.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Chord}{The straight-line distance from the leading edge to the trailing edge of an airfoil.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Chord Line}{The imaginary line connecting the leading and trailing edges.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Circulation}{The line integral of velocity around a closed curve, proportional to lift.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Clean Configuration}{The flight configuration of an aircraft in which all high-lift devices (flaps, slats) and landing gear are fully retracted, minimizing drag and maximizing airspeed for a given power setting. Clean configuration is used for cruise, climb, and high-speed flight.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Closed Circuit Tunnel}{A wind tunnel with a continuous return circuit.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Cold Structure}{An aerospace structural design approach in which the primary load-bearing structure is protected from high temperatures by external insulation, maintaining it at relatively low temperatures. Cold structures allow the use of conventional materials like aluminum but add insulation weight.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Combustion Noise}{The noise generated by unsteady heat release and turbulent mixing in the combustion chamber of a gas turbine engine. Combustion noise contributes to the overall engine noise signature and is influenced by fuel-air mixing and combustor geometry.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Compressible Flow}{Flow in which density variations are significant.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Compressor}{A device increasing the pressure of a gas.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Computational Fluid Dynamics}{Numerical methods to solve fluid flow problems.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Continuity Equation}{Mass conservation in fluid flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Contra-Rotating Propeller}{Two coaxial propellers rotating in opposite directions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Contraction Cone}{The converging section of a wind tunnel that accelerates the flow from the settling chamber to the test section. The contraction ratio (area ratio from inlet to outlet) determines the velocity increase and flow uniformity, with higher ratios producing lower turbulence levels.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Contrail}{A condensation trail from aircraft engine exhaust.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Control Reversal}{Loss or reversal of control effectiveness at high speeds.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Convective Heating}{The component of aerodynamic heating resulting from the transfer of thermal energy from the hot boundary layer gas to the vehicle surface through conduction and convection. Convective heating dominates at moderate re-entry velocities and is influenced by boundary layer state (laminar or turbulent).}{catAD}
\dictentry{Coordinated Turn}{A turn with no sideslip, balanced lift and centripetal force.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Corner Vanes}{Curved guide vanes installed in the corners of closed-circuit wind tunnels to turn the flow with minimal pressure loss and flow separation. They are essential for maintaining flow quality and reducing the power required to drive the tunnel.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Critical Angle of Attack}{The angle of attack at which the wing stalls.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Critical Mach Number}{The freestream Mach at which sonic flow first appears on the airframe.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Cruciform Tail}{A tail with horizontal and vertical surfaces forming a cross.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Cryogenic Wind Tunnel}{A tunnel using cold nitrogen gas to increase Reynolds number.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Decalage}{The angular difference between the chord lines of the upper and lower wings of a biplane, measured in degrees. Positive decalage (upper wing at a higher angle of incidence) results in greater lift from the upper wing and is used to tailor stall characteristics and longitudinal stability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Deceleron}{A split aileron that also acts as an airbrake.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Delta Wing}{A triangular planform wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Detached Shock}{A shock wave not in contact with the body.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Diffuser}{A duct that decelerates flow, increasing its pressure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Diffuser Section}{The expanding section of a wind tunnel located downstream of the test section, designed to decelerate the flow and recover static pressure. A well-designed diffuser minimizes pressure losses and improves overall tunnel efficiency.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dihedral}{The upward angle of wings increasing lateral stability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dimple}{A surface indentation used for drag reduction, as on golf balls.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Disk Loading}{The average pressure change across an actuator disk such as a propeller or rotor, defined as the thrust divided by the disk area. Low disk loading is characteristic of rotors and high-lift propellers, while high disk loading is typical of high-speed propellers and ducted fans.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Displacement Thickness}{The outward displacement of external flow by boundary layer slowing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Divergence}{An instability where a disturbance increases in magnitude.}{catAD}
\dictentry{DNS}{Direct Numerical Simulation, solving all turbulent scales directly.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dogtooth}{A notch in the leading edge to generate vortices.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Doublet}{A potential flow element combining a source and sink.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Downwash}{The downward deflection of air behind a wing, contributing to induced drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Drag}{The aerodynamic force opposing motion through the air.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Drag Coefficient}{A dimensionless coefficient relating drag to dynamic pressure and area.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dutch Roll}{A coupled lateral-directional oscillation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dynamic Stability}{The evolution of aircraft motion over time after a disturbance.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dynamic Stall}{A delayed stall from rapid change in angle of attack, with temporary vortex lift.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Dynamic Testing}{Testing with time-varying conditions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Electric Propulsion}{Aircraft propulsion systems that use electric motors powered by batteries, fuel cells, or hybrid generators to drive propellers or fans. Electric propulsion enables distributed propulsion configurations, reduces local emissions, and opens new vehicle architectures for urban air mobility.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Elevator}{A horizontal control surface on the tail that controls pitch.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Elevon}{A combined elevator and aileron control surface on tailless aircraft.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Elliptical Wing}{A wing with elliptical planform, giving minimum induced drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Emission Index}{A quantitative measure of pollutant mass produced per unit mass of fuel burned in an aircraft engine, expressed in grams of pollutant per kilogram of fuel. Emission indices for NOx, CO, unburned hydrocarbons, and soot are used to assess engine environmental performance.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Endplate}{A flat plate at the wingtip to reduce induced drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Energy Equation}{Energy conservation in fluid flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Enstrophy}{A quantity in fluid dynamics that measures the integrated square of the vorticity magnitude, representing the dissipation of kinetic energy in turbulent flows. Enstrophy is particularly useful in the study of turbulence and is directly related to the turbulent kinetic energy dissipation rate.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Euler Equations}{The Navier-Stokes equations without viscosity and thermal terms.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Expansion Fan}{A fan of expansion waves around a convex corner.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Expansion Wave}{A wave that expands and accelerates supersonic flow around a convex corner.}{catAD}
\dictentry{External Balance}{A balance mounted outside the wind tunnel.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Fan Noise}{The noise produced by the fan stage of turbofan engines, comprising tonal noise at the blade passage frequency and broadband noise from turbulent interactions. Fan noise is a significant contributor to aircraft noise during takeoff and approach.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Fence}{A vertical surface on a wing to control spanwise flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Finite Element Method}{A numerical technique for approximate boundary value solutions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Finite Volume Method}{A numerical method using control volumes to solve PDEs.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Fish-Tail Shock}{A shock pattern resembling a fish tail.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flap}{A high-lift device on the trailing edge that increases camber and lift.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flapperon}{A combined flap and aileron.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flexible TPS}{A lightweight, conformable thermal protection material made of ceramic or polymer fibers, used for areas with moderate heating on re-entry vehicles. Flexible TPS blankets can be draped over curved surfaces and are easier to install than rigid tiles.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flow Angularity}{The deviation of flow direction from the tunnel axis.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flow Separation}{Detachment of boundary layer from a surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flutter}{An aeroelastic oscillation that can be destructive.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Flying Wing}{A tailless aircraft where the wing is the primary structure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Force Balance}{A device measuring aerodynamic forces on a wind tunnel model.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Form Drag}{Drag due to the shape of the body.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Forward Swept Wing}{A wing angled forward from the root.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Friction Drag}{Drag due to shear stress between the surface and the fluid.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Full Model Testing}{Testing a complete model.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Geometric Twist}{Physical twist of the wing structure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Grid}{The discretized representation of a fluid domain.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Grid Turbulence}{Turbulence generated by a grid in the tunnel.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Ground Effect}{Reduced induced drag when flying close to the ground.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Gust Load}{A transient load from atmospheric turbulence.}{catAD}
\dictentry{H-Tail}{A twin vertical tail configuration.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Half Model Testing}{Testing a half-span model against a wall.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Heat Flux}{The rate of thermal energy transfer per unit area, expressed in watts per square meter (W/m2), at a surface exposed to aerodynamic heating. Heat flux is a critical parameter in the design of thermal protection systems and is composed of convective, radiative, and catalytic components.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Heat Shield}{The forward-facing component of an aeroshell that absorbs and dissipates the intense heat generated during atmospheric entry. Heat shields may be ablative (consumed during entry) or reusable (ceramic tiles) depending on the mission requirements.}{catAD}
\dictentry{High Altitude Flight}{Flight operations at altitudes above 50,000 feet, where low air density requires high aerodynamic efficiency and specialized propulsion systems. High altitude flight includes stratospheric platforms for communications, surveillance, and atmospheric science.}{catAD}
\dictentry{High Speed Tunnel}{A wind tunnel for transonic and supersonic testing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hoerner Tip}{A rounded, drooped wingtip design for drag reduction.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Honeycomb Straightener}{A flow conditioning device consisting of a bundle of small-diameter tubes or cells placed upstream of the contraction cone in a wind tunnel. It reduces turbulence and swirl by breaking up large-scale eddies and aligning the flow with the tunnel axis, improving flow quality in the test section.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Horseshoe Vortex}{The vortex system modeled as bound vortex plus trailing vortices.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hot Structure}{An aerospace structural design philosophy in which the primary load-bearing structure is allowed to reach high temperatures during flight, eliminating the need for heavy insulation. Hot structures are typically made of high-temperature alloys, titanium, or ceramic matrix composites.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hot Wire Anemometry}{A measurement technique using heated wires for velocity.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hush Kit}{A set of modifications applied to an aircraft engine or nacelle to reduce noise emissions, typically consisting of acoustic liners, exhaust mixers, and nozzle treatments. Hush kits allow older aircraft to meet modern noise certification standards.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hybrid Propulsion}{A propulsion architecture combining a conventional thermal engine (turbine or piston) with an electric motor and battery system. Hybrid propulsion allows the thermal engine to operate at optimum efficiency while the electric motor provides peak power for takeoff and climb.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hydrogen Propulsion}{The use of hydrogen as a fuel for aircraft propulsion, either burned in modified gas turbine engines or used in fuel cells to power electric motors. Hydrogen offers zero CO2 emissions at the point of use but presents challenges in storage volume, cryogenic handling, and infrastructure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hypersonic Flow}{Flow with Mach number greater than 5.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Hypersonic Wind Tunnel}{A wind tunnel for Mach numbers above five.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Incompressible Flow}{Flow in which density is assumed constant.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Indraft Wind Tunnel}{A tunnel drawing air from the room through the test section.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Induced Drag}{Drag created as a consequence of producing lift.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Induced Velocity}{The downward velocity component imparted to the air by a lifting wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Inflatable TPS}{A deployable thermal protection system consisting of a flexible, inflatable aeroshell that decelerates a payload during atmospheric entry. Inflatable TPS enables larger drag areas than rigid aeroshells of the same mass, reducing peak heating and deceleration.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Infrared Thermography}{An imaging technique for surface temperature measurement.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Insulation}{Materials with low thermal conductivity placed between the hot outer surface and the underlying structure to reduce heat transfer. In aerospace applications, fibrous insulation blankets, aerogels, and multilayer insulation are used to protect sensitive components from high temperatures.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Interference Drag}{Drag from the interaction of flow fields between different components.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Internal Balance}{A balance mounted inside the model.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Inviscid Flow}{An idealized flow neglecting viscosity effects.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Irrotational Flow}{Flow in which fluid particles have zero net rotation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Jet Noise}{The noise generated by the high-velocity exhaust of a jet engine, resulting from turbulent mixing of the jet plume with the ambient air. Jet noise is a major component of aircraft noise during takeoff and is reduced through chevron nozzles and serrated ejectors.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Joukowski Airfoil}{An airfoil shape generated by conformal mapping.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Keel Effect}{The aerodynamic phenomenon where lateral area located behind the center of gravity provides directional stability, analogous to the keel of a ship. In aircraft design, the keel effect is provided by the vertical tail and aft fuselage and contributes to weathercock stability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Kutta Condition}{The principle that flow leaves the sharp trailing edge smoothly.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Kutta-Joukowski Theorem}{Lift per unit span equals density times velocity times circulation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lambda Shock}{A lambda-shaped shock in transonic flow over airfoils.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Laminar Flow}{Smooth, orderly fluid motion with distinct parallel layers.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Laminar Flow Control}{Active methods to maintain laminar boundary layer.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Laser Doppler Velocimetry}{An optical velocity measurement technique.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lateral Stability}{Stability about the longitudinal axis, including roll.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Leading Edge}{The forward edge of an airfoil or wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Leading Edge Extension}{A strake extending forward from the wing root.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Leading Edge TPS}{The thermal protection system applied to the leading edges of wings, fins, and control surfaces of re-entry vehicles and hypersonic aircraft. Leading edge TPS must withstand high heat fluxes while maintaining aerodynamic shape and structural integrity.}{catAD}
\dictentry{LES}{Large Eddy Simulation, solving for large-scale turbulent eddies.}{catAD}
\dictentry{LEX Fence}{A fence on the leading edge extension for vortex control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lift}{The aerodynamic force perpendicular to the relative wind supporting the aircraft.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lift Coefficient}{A dimensionless coefficient relating lift to dynamic pressure and area.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lift Distribution}{Spanwise variation of lift on a wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lifting Line Theory}{Prandtl's classical aerodynamic theory for finite wings that models the wing as a bound vortex line with trailing vortices forming a vortex sheet. The theory predicts spanwise lift distribution, induced drag, and downwash, and shows that an elliptical lift distribution produces minimum induced drag. It provides the theoretical basis for understanding finite wing aerodynamics and wing design.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lifting Re-entry}{A re-entry trajectory that uses aerodynamic lift generated by the vehicle body or wings to control the descent path and reduce peak deceleration. Lifting re-entry, used by the Space Shuttle and proposed for crewed Mars missions, enables cross-range maneuvering and gentler deceleration.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Lifting Surface Theory}{A more general theory for wings of arbitrary planform.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Load Factor}{The ratio of lift to weight, measured in g.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Longitudinal Stability}{Stability about the lateral axis concerning pitch motion.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Low Speed Tunnel}{A wind tunnel for Mach numbers below 0.3.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mach Angle}{The angle of the Mach cone: mu = arcsin(1/M).}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mach Cone}{The cone-shaped wavefront from a supersonic body.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mach Number}{The ratio of flow speed to the local speed of sound.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mach Tuck}{A nose-down pitching moment at transonic speeds.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mach Wave}{A weak wave at the Mach angle in supersonic flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Magnetic Suspension}{A model support system using magnetic fields.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Maneuverability}{The ability of an aircraft to change direction quickly.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Maneuvering Load}{Load from pilot-induced maneuvers.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mean Aerodynamic Chord}{The chord of an equivalent rectangular wing with identical moment characteristics.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mean Line}{Another term for the camber line of an airfoil.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Mesh}{The cells or nodes forming a discretized fluid volume.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Method of Characteristics}{A mathematical method for solving supersonic flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Momentum Equation}{Newton's second law applied to fluid motion, stating that the net force acting on a fluid element equals the rate of change of its momentum. In integral form, it relates the sum of surface and body forces to the time rate of change of momentum within a control volume plus the net flux of momentum across its boundaries. The momentum equation is fundamental in aerodynamic force analysis and propulsion system design.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Momentum Thickness}{A measure of the momentum deficit in the boundary layer.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Natural Laminar Flow}{A design approach to maintain laminar boundary layer over more of the wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Navier-Stokes Equations}{The fundamental equations describing viscous fluid motion.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Neutral Point}{The point where pitching moment is independent of angle of attack.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Noise Certification}{The regulatory process by which aircraft are certified to meet maximum allowable noise levels established by aviation authorities such as ICAO, FAA, and EASA. Noise certification defines measurement procedures, flight test conditions, and noise limits for takeoff, approach, and sideline.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Noise Reduction}{Techniques and technologies employed to reduce aircraft noise, including engine nacelle treatments, chevron nozzles, acoustic liners, shielding by airframe structures, and operational procedures such as continuous descent approaches.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Normal Shock}{A shock wave perpendicular to the flow direction.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Nose Cap}{The forward-most component of a re-entry vehicle or hypersonic aircraft, exposed to the highest stagnation temperatures and pressures. The nose cap is typically made of reinforced carbon-carbon or an ablative material and must withstand extreme thermal and mechanical loads.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Nozzle}{A duct that accelerates a fluid to produce thrust.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Oblique Shock}{A shock wave inclined to the direction of supersonic flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Ogive}{A pointed, curved shape used for nose cones.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Open Circuit Tunnel}{A wind tunnel with no return circuit.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Optical Strain Measurement}{A non-contact technique using cameras to measure deformation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Oscillatory Testing}{Testing with oscillating model or flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Oswald Span Efficiency}{A factor relating induced drag to that of an elliptical wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Panel Method}{A numerical potential flow method using surface panels.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Parasite Drag}{All drag not associated with lift production.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Particle Image Velocimetry}{An optical flow measurement technique using particle imaging.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pathline}{The trajectory traced by a fluid particle over time.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Peak Heating}{The maximum instantaneous heating rate experienced by a spacecraft during atmospheric re-entry, occurring when the combination of velocity and atmospheric density produces the highest convective and radiative heat flux. Peak heating determines the required thickness and material of the thermal protection system.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Phase Doppler Anemometry}{A technique measuring particle size and velocity.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Phugoid}{A long-period longitudinal oscillation exchanging kinetic and potential energy.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pitching Moment}{The aerodynamic moment about the lateral axis.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pitot Tube}{A tube facing the flow to measure stagnation pressure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Prandtl Number}{The ratio of momentum diffusivity to thermal diffusivity.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Prandtl-Meyer Expansion}{Isentropic expansion of supersonic flow around a convex corner.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Prandtl-Meyer Function}{The angle function for Prandtl-Meyer expansion.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pressure Coefficient}{A dimensionless pressure normalized by dynamic pressure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pressure Drag}{Drag from the pressure difference between front and rear of a body.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pressure Scanner}{A multi-port pressure measurement system.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pressure Sensitive Paint}{A paint that fluoresces in proportion to surface pressure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Propeller}{A rotating airfoil that converts engine power into thrust.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Propeller Noise}{The noise generated by rotating propellers due to blade loading, thickness effects, and tip vortices. It consists of tonal components at the blade passage frequency and broadband noise from turbulent boundary layer interactions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Pulsed Detonation Engine}{An engine using detonation waves for propulsion.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Radiative Heating}{The component of aerodynamic heating resulting from thermal radiation emitted by the high-temperature shock layer gas, particularly at hypersonic velocities above 10 km/s. Radiative heating becomes dominant at very high re-entry velocities and is a major consideration for Mars return and planetary entry.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Raked Wingtip}{A wingtip with a swept trailing edge.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Ramjet}{An air-breathing engine using forward motion for compression.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rankine Body}{A streamlined body shape from source-sink superposition.}{catAD}
\dictentry{RANS}{Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes, a time-averaged CFD approach.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rate of Turn}{The angular velocity of an aircraft in a turn.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Re-entry}{The return of a spacecraft to the atmosphere.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Re-entry Angle}{The angle between the flight path of a re-entering vehicle and the local horizontal at the point of atmospheric entry. A steep re-entry angle increases peak heating rates and deceleration, while a shallow angle extends the heating duration and may cause skip-out.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Re-entry Corridor}{The allowable range of flight path angles within which a re-entering spacecraft must fly to achieve a safe landing at the intended site. Deviations outside the corridor result in excessive heating, structural failure, or landing far from the target.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Re-entry Heating Rate}{The rate at which thermal energy is transferred to a spacecraft surface during atmospheric re-entry, typically expressed in watts per square centimeter (W/cm2). The peak heating rate occurs at the point of maximum deceleration and drives thermal protection system design.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Re-entry Velocity}{The speed at which a spacecraft enters a planetary atmosphere, typically between 7.8 km/s (low Earth orbit) and 11.2 km/s (escape velocity from Earth). The re-entry velocity determines the kinetic energy that must be dissipated as heat during atmospheric passage.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Reattachment}{The re-establishment of attached flow after separation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Reinforced Carbon Carbon}{A composite material consisting of carbon fibers embedded in a carbon matrix, capable of withstanding temperatures up to 3000F (1650C). Reinforced carbon-carbon is used for the highest-temperature areas of re-entry vehicles, including nose caps and wing leading edges.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Relative Wind}{The direction of airflow relative to a moving airfoil.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Reusable TPS}{A thermal protection system designed to withstand multiple re-entry cycles without replacement, typically using ceramic tiles, fibrous insulation blankets, or metallic panels. Reusable TPS was developed for the Space Shuttle and is essential for affordable reusable launch vehicles.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Reynolds Analogy}{Relation between heat transfer and skin friction.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Reynolds Number}{The ratio of inertial to viscous forces in a flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Riblet}{Microgrooves on a surface that reduce skin friction drag.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rogallo Wing}{A flexible, delta-shaped wing design consisting of two conically shaped fabric surfaces held taut by a rigid frame or inflated tubes. Rogallo wings are used in hang gliders, parawings, and some spacecraft recovery systems for their light weight and deployability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rolling Moment}{The aerodynamic moment about the longitudinal axis.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rotor}{A rotating wing assembly that generates lift and propulsion.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rotor Noise}{The acoustic signature of helicopter main rotors and tail rotors, including blade-vortex interaction noise, high-speed impulsive noise, and broadband turbulence ingestion noise. Rotor noise is a critical consideration for urban air mobility and military operations.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Rudder}{A vertical control surface on the tail that controls yaw.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Schlieren Photography}{An optical technique for imaging density gradients in flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Scramjet}{A supersonic ramjet with supersonic internal flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Screens}{Fine-mesh grids placed in the settling chamber of a wind tunnel to reduce turbulence intensity and improve flow uniformity. Multiple screens with decreasing mesh size are often used in series to achieve high-quality flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Separated Flow}{Flow that has detached from the surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Settling Chamber}{The wide-section region of a wind tunnel located between the flow conditioning screens and the contraction cone. It provides a low-velocity environment where turbulence decays and flow uniformity is established before acceleration into the test section.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Shock Diamond}{Visible diamond-shaped patterns in supersonic exhaust plumes.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Shock Layer}{The hot shocked gas between the bow shock and vehicle surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Shock Stall}{Stall caused by shock-induced boundary layer separation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Shock Tunnel}{A tunnel using shock waves to produce high-enthalpy flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Shock Wave}{A thin region across which flow properties change abruptly.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Shock-Free Airfoil}{An airfoil designed to avoid shock waves at design conditions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Short Period Mode}{A well-damped, high-frequency longitudinal oscillation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Sideslip}{The angle between an aircraft's longitudinal axis and the relative wind direction, indicating a lateral component of motion relative to the airflow. Sideslip affects the distribution of aerodynamic forces on the fuselage and vertical tail, and is a critical parameter in lateral-directional stability analysis, crosswind landings, and coordinated turn calculations.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Skin Friction}{The shear resistance between the surface and the fluid.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Skin Friction Drag}{Drag due to shear stress on the surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Skip Re-entry}{A re-entry trajectory in which the vehicle descends into the upper atmosphere, generates aerodynamic lift to ascend back out of the atmosphere, then re-enters a second time. Skip re-entry extends the range and allows higher entry velocities while managing peak heating.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Slat}{A leading-edge high-lift device that extends to increase maximum lift.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Slender Body Theory}{Theory for long, thin bodies at small angles of attack.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Smoke Visualization}{Using smoke to visualize flow patterns over a model.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Solar Powered Flight}{Aircraft propulsion using photovoltaic cells mounted on the wing and fuselage surfaces to convert sunlight into electrical energy for motors and batteries. Solar powered aircraft can achieve sustained flight at high altitudes for extended durations, limited by energy storage and diurnal cycles.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Sonic Boom}{The audible shock wave from a supersonic aircraft reaching the ground.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Source Panel Method}{A panel method using source/sink distributions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Spoiler}{A surface that disrupts lift and increases drag for roll control or speed reduction.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Spoileron}{A spoiler used differentially for roll control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Stabilator}{A one-piece horizontal tail that pivots for pitch control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Stagnation Point}{A point on a body where the flow velocity is zero.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Stagnation Pressure}{The pressure at a stagnation point.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Stall}{Flow separation from the wing due to excessive angle of attack.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Starting Vortex}{The vortex shed when a wing begins moving.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Static Margin}{The distance between the neutral point and CG, determining static stability.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Static Port}{An opening used to measure static pressure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Static Pressure}{The ambient pressure of the flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Static Stability}{The initial tendency to return to equilibrium after a disturbance.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Static Test}{Structural testing under predetermined loads.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Static Testing}{Testing under steady conditions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Steady Flow}{Flow where properties at a point are constant over time.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Sting Mount}{A support strut holding a model from behind.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Strain Gauge}{A sensor measuring strain on a surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Strake}{A narrow extension to a wing or fuselage for aerodynamic effect.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Strakes}{A sharp-edged surface for vortex generation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Stratospheric Flight}{Flight within the stratosphere (approximately 35,000 to 160,000 feet), characterized by stable atmospheric conditions, low turbulence, and minimal weather. Stratospheric platforms enable long-endurance missions for earth observation, telecommunications, and scientific research.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Streakline}{The locus of particles passing through a fixed point.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Streamline}{A line tangent to the velocity vector at every point in a flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Strut Mount}{A support structure holding a model from below or above.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Suborbital Flight}{A trajectory that reaches space (above the Karman line at 100 km altitude) but lacks the velocity to achieve orbit, resulting in a ballistic return to Earth. Suborbital flight is used for sounding rockets, crewed space tourism, and hypersonic research.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Subsonic Flow}{Flow with Mach number less than 1.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Supercritical Airfoil}{An airfoil designed to delay drag rise at transonic speeds.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Supersonic Flow}{Flow with Mach number greater than 1.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Supersonic Wind Tunnel}{A wind tunnel designed for Mach numbers above one.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Sustainable Aviation Fuel}{A liquid fuel derived from renewable or waste feedstocks such as used cooking oil, agricultural residues, or municipal waste, designed to be a drop-in replacement for conventional jet fuel. Sustainable aviation fuel can reduce lifecycle CO2 emissions by up to 80\% compared to fossil-based jet fuel.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Sweep Angle}{The angle between the wing quarter-chord line and the lateral axis.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Sweep Theory}{Simplified method for swept wing aerodynamics.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Swept Wing}{A wing angled rearward from the root.}{catAD}
\dictentry{T-Tail}{A tail with the horizontal surface at the top of the vertical fin.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Taileron}{A stabilator used differentially for roll control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Taper Ratio}{The ratio of tip chord to root chord.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Temperature Sensitive Paint}{A paint indicating surface temperature.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Test Section}{The wind tunnel area where models are placed in uniform flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thermal Barrier Coating}{A thin layer of ceramic material applied to metallic components to reduce heat transfer and protect against oxidation and thermal fatigue. Thermal barrier coatings are used on turbine blades, combustion chambers, and exhaust nozzles in gas turbine engines.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thermal Protection Material}{Materials specifically engineered to withstand the extreme heat loads of atmospheric re-entry or hypersonic flight. Thermal protection materials include ablative composites (carbon-phenolic), reusable ceramics (tiles), and advanced carbon-carbon composites, each selected for specific temperature ranges and mission profiles.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thermal Protection System}{Materials protecting a spacecraft from re-entry heating.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thermocouple}{A temperature sensor used in aerodynamic testing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thickness Ratio}{The ratio of maximum thickness to chord of an airfoil.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thin Airfoil Theory}{Linearized theory predicting airfoil lift and moment.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thrust}{The propulsive force moving the aircraft forward.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Thrust Vectoring}{The ability to redirect engine thrust for attitude control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Tiles}{Rigid ceramic insulating blocks used as reusable thermal protection on the Space Shuttle and other re-entry vehicles. Tiles are made of high-purity silica fibers and are coated with a borosilicate glass coating for waterproofing and emissivity control.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Tip Fence}{A small vertical surface at the wingtip.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Total Pressure}{The sum of static and dynamic pressure.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Trailing Edge}{The aft edge of an airfoil or wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Trailing Vortex}{The vortex rolling up from the wingtip.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Transitional Flow}{Flow between laminar and turbulent states.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Transonic Flow}{Flow in the Mach 0.8-1.2 range with mixed subsonic/supersonic regions.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Trim Drag}{Drag from deflecting control surfaces to maintain trimmed flight.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turbine}{A rotary device extracting energy from flowing fluid.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turbofan}{A ducted jet engine where a fan provides a large portion of thrust.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turbojet}{A gas turbine engine producing thrust by accelerating a jet rearward.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turbulence Intensity}{A measure of the fluctuation level in wind tunnel flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turbulence Model}{A model for Reynolds stresses in turbulent CFD.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turbulent Flow}{Chaotic, irregular fluid motion characterized by eddies.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turning Radius}{The radius of a turn at a given speed and bank angle.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Turning Vanes}{Aerodynamic surfaces placed in duct bends or wind tunnel corners to redirect airflow efficiently. They prevent flow separation and reduce total pressure losses by guiding the flow around turns.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Twin Tail}{Two separate vertical fins.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Unsteady Flow}{Flow where properties at a point vary with time.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Upwash}{The upward flow ahead of a wing that influences surfaces in front.}{catAD}
\dictentry{V-n Diagram}{A plot of airspeed versus load factor defining flight envelope.}{catAD}
\dictentry{V-Tail}{A tail configuration with two angled surfaces combining rudder and elevator.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Vapor Cone}{A visible cone of condensed water vapor around an aircraft at transonic speeds.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Viscosity}{A measure of a fluid's resistance to shear deformation, arising from intermolecular forces and momentum transfer between fluid layers. In aerodynamics, viscosity governs the behavior of the boundary layer, determines skin friction drag, and influences flow separation, transition to turbulence, and heat transfer.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Vortex}{A region of rotating fluid, often forming at wingtips.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Vortex Generator}{A small vane that energizes the boundary layer to delay separation.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Vortex Lattice Method}{A numerical method modeling surfaces with vortex rings.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Vortex Shedding}{Alternating vortices shed from a bluff body in a flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wake}{The region of disturbed flow behind a body.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wall Interference}{The effect of wind tunnel walls on measured results.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wall Shear Stress}{The frictional stress exerted by the flow on the surface.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wash-In}{A wing twist increasing angle of attack at the tip.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Washout}{A wing twist that reduces angle of attack at the tip relative to the root.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wave Drag}{Drag from shock wave formation in transonic and supersonic flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wind Tunnel}{An experimental facility generating controlled airflow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wind Tunnel Fan}{The fan driving airflow in a wind tunnel.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing}{The primary lifting surface of an aircraft.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing Area}{The planform surface area of the wing used in lift/drag calculations.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing Fence}{A fence on the upper wing surface to prevent spanwise flow.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing Loading}{The ratio of aircraft weight to wing area.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing Root}{The junction where the wing meets the fuselage.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing Span}{The distance from one wingtip to the other.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wing Tip}{The extreme outer end of the wing.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Winglet}{A vertical tip device that reduces induced drag by modifying the tip vortex.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Wingtip Vortex}{The spiral vortex shed from a wingtip.}{catAD}
\dictentry{Yawing Moment}{The aerodynamic moment about the vertical axis.}{catAD}

% ===== AEROSPACE TERMS =====
\dictentry{Ablative Material}{A material eroding to carry away heat.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Acceptance Test}{Production unit tests verifying specifications.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Active Flow Control}{Active methods for manipulating flow.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Additive Manufacturing}{Building objects layer by layer.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Aerodynamic Balance}{A surface shape reducing control forces.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Aerospace Engineering}{The primary field covering aircraft and spacecraft development.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Afterburner}{A secondary combustion chamber for thrust augmentation.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Air Conditioning}{A system controlling cabin temperature.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Air Data Computer}{A computer computing altitude, speed, and Mach number.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Aircraft}{Any vehicle designed to travel through the air.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Aircraft Wiring}{Electrical wiring in aircraft systems.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Airlock}{A chamber maintaining pressure integrity during passage.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Airplane}{A powered fixed-wing aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Airship}{A powered, steerable lighter-than-air craft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Airworthiness}{Suitability for safe flight.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Alternator}{An AC generator in aircraft electrical systems.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Aluminum Alloy}{Lightweight alloy extensively used in aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Anti-Servo Tab}{A tab moving with the control surface for feel.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Anti-Skid System}{A safety system preventing wheel lock during braking.}{catAE}
\dictentry{APU}{Auxiliary Power Unit, a small engine for ground power.}{catAE}
\dictentry{ARINC 429}{A standard for avionics data communication.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Attitude Control}{Controlling the orientation of a vehicle.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Attitude Determination}{Calculating vehicle orientation from sensor data.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Autoclave Curing}{Composite curing with heat and pressure in an autoclave.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Autonomous Flight}{Flight without human intervention.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Autopilot}{A system automatically controlling the flight path.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Autorotation Landing}{A landing without engine power.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Autothrottle}{An automatic system controlling engine thrust to maintain speed.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Balance Tab}{A tab that moves opposite to the control surface.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Balloon}{An unpowered lighter-than-air craft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Battery}{An electrochemical power storage device.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Bellerank}{A lever changing the direction of control movement.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Bleed Air}{Compressed air from the engine for pneumatic systems.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Blown Flap}{A flap with engine bleed air for boundary layer control.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Boosted Controls}{Power-assisted flight controls.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Booster}{The first stage providing initial thrust.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Brake}{A device for slowing or stopping the aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Braking System}{Components decelerating the aircraft on the runway.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Bulkhead}{A transverse partition carrying concentrated loads.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Bus Bar}{A power distribution conductor.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cabin Pressurization}{Maintaining safe cabin altitude at high flight levels.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cable}{A wire cable for control transmission.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Calibrated Airspeed}{IAS corrected for instrument and position errors.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Canopy}{The transparent enclosure over the cockpit.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Carbon Fiber}{A strong, stiff, lightweight composite material.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cat A Takeoff}{A takeoff with one engine failure capability.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cat B Takeoff}{A takeoff assuming no engine failure.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Caution Warning System}{A system alerting the crew to abnormal conditions.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Ceiling}{The maximum altitude an aircraft can reach.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Ceramic Matrix Composite}{A composite with ceramic matrix for high temperatures.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Circuit Breaker}{A resettable overcurrent protection device.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Circular Orbit}{An orbit with eccentricity of 0.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Circulation Control Wing}{A wing using blowing for lift control.}{catAE}
\dictentry{CNC Machining}{Computer numerical controlled automated machining.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cockpit}{The compartment from which the pilot controls the aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Collective}{The control for helicopter rotor pitch.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Collective Pitch}{Simultaneous pitch change of all rotor blades.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Combustion Chamber}{The engine section where fuel and air burn.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Composite Material}{A material combining two or more constituent materials.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Control Column}{The primary flight control input device.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Control Horn}{An arm on a control surface for linkage attachment.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Control Law}{A mathematical equation determining control outputs.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Control Surface}{A movable surface for aerodynamic control.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cosmic Ray}{High-energy particles from outer space.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cyclic}{The control stick for helicopter rotor tilt.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Cyclic Pitch}{Cyclic variation of blade pitch for rotor tilt.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Data Bus}{A communication network for aircraft systems.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Dead Reckoning}{Position estimation from speed, time, and direction.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Delta-v}{The velocity change capacity for orbital maneuvers.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Digital Twin}{A virtual model of a physical system.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Dissymmetry of Lift}{Unequal lift between advancing and retreating blades.}{catAE}
\dictentry{DO-178C}{Guidelines for aeronautical software development.}{catAE}
\dictentry{DO-254}{Guidelines for complex aeronautical hardware development.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Docking Port}{An interface for connecting two spacecraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Downwash}{Air deflected downward by a wing in producing lift.}{catAE}
\dictentry{EASA}{European Union Aviation Safety Agency.}{catAE}
\dictentry{ECAM}{Electronic Centralized Aircraft Monitor.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Eccentricity}{A measure of orbital deviation from circular.}{catAE}
\dictentry{ECS Pack}{An environmental control system unit.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Effective Translational Lift}{Lift increase as the rotor leaves its downwash.}{catAE}
\dictentry{EICAS}{Engine Indicating and Crew Alerting System.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Electric Aircraft}{Aircraft powered by electric motors and batteries.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Electrical System}{The system providing electrical power to the aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Electron Beam Welding}{A welding process using high-velocity electrons.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Elliptical Orbit}{An orbit with eccentricity between 0 and 1.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Empennage}{The tail assembly providing stability and control.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Engine Display}{A display showing engine parameters.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Environmental Control System}{The system managing cabin pressure, temperature, and ventilation.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Escape Velocity}{Speed needed to break free from gravity.}{catAE}
\dictentry{eVTOL}{An electric vertical takeoff and landing aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{FAA}{Federal Aviation Administration, US aviation authority.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fail-Safe Design}{Design preventing or mitigating failure consequences.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fairing}{The nose cone protecting the payload during ascent.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fatigue Test}{Testing under repeated cyclic loads.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fault Tolerance}{Continued operation after component failures.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Feathering Hinge}{A hinge allowing blade pitch change.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fenestron}{A ducted tail rotor.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fiberglass}{Glass fibers embedded in polymer matrix.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Filament Winding}{Winding resin-impregnated fibers around a mandrel.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fin}{Another term for the vertical stabilizer.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flapping Hinge}{A hinge allowing blade vertical movement.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Computer}{The central computing unit for flight data.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Director}{A flight instrument displaying command guidance cues to the pilot.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Management System}{An integrated computer for navigation and performance.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Mechanics}{The study of forces and motion of aircraft in flight.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Path}{The trajectory of the aircraft's center of gravity.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Safety}{Ensuring safe aircraft or spacecraft operation.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flight Test}{Testing in actual flight conditions.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flow Separation}{Boundary layer detachment causing lift loss.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Flutter Test}{Testing for aeroelastic oscillation speeds.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fly-by-Wire}{Electrical transmission of pilot commands.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fowler Flap}{A flap extending aft and down for increased area and camber.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Friction Stir Welding}{A solid-state joining process using frictional heat.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fuel System}{The system storing and delivering fuel to engines.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fully Articulated Rotor}{A rotor with all three hinges.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Fuselage}{The central body of an aircraft.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Generator}{A device converting mechanical to electrical power.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Geostationary Orbit}{A circular orbit at 35,786 km synchronized to Earth's rotation.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Glass Cockpit}{A cockpit with electronic flight instrument displays.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Glide Ratio}{Horizontal distance per altitude lost, equal to L/D.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Glider}{A fixed-wing aircraft without an engine.}{catAE}
\dictentry{GNSS}{Global Navigation Satellite Systems: GPS, GLONASS, Galileo, BeiDou.}{catAE}
\dictentry{GPS}{Satellite-based global positioning system.}{catAE}
\dictentry{GPWS}{Ground Proximity Warning System alerting the crew of terrain hazards.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Gravity Turn}{Using gravity to pitch the vehicle during ascent.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Great Circle Route}{The shortest path between two points on a sphere.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Ground Effect Helicopter}{Increased lift near the ground.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Ground Resonance}{A destructive oscillation of rotor and airframe.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Ground Speed}{Speed relative to the ground.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Guidance}{Determining the desired path for a vehicle.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Gust Alleviation}{Active control reducing gust loads.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Gyrocopter}{A rotorcraft with an unpowered rotor in autorotation.}{catAE}
\dictentry{H-V Curve}{The height-velocity diagram for helicopters.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Handley Page Slat}{A leading edge slat that extends forward.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hazard Analysis}{Identification of potential hazards.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Head-Up Display}{A transparent display showing flight data in the pilot's line of sight.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Heat Shield}{A layer dissipating re-entry thermal energy.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Height-Velocity Diagram}{A diagram showing safe hover and landing regions.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Helicopter}{A rotary-wing aircraft with one or more horizontal rotors.}{catAE}
\dictentry{High-Lift Device}{A device increasing lift at low speeds.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hohmann Transfer}{An efficient elliptical transfer between circular orbits.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Honeycomb Panel}{A lightweight composite panel with a honeycomb core.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Horizontal Stabilizer}{The fixed horizontal tail surface for longitudinal stability.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Horn Balance}{An extension forward of the hinge line.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hot Staging}{Upper stage ignition before lower stage separation.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hover}{Flight at zero ground speed.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hybrid-Electric Propulsion}{Combining conventional and electric propulsion.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hydraulic System}{Pressurized fluid system for actuating flight controls.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hydrogen Aircraft}{Aircraft using hydrogen as fuel.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Hypersonic Vehicle}{A craft capable of sustained flight above Mach 5.}{catAE}
\dictentry{ICAO}{International Civil Aviation Organization.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Ice Protection System}{A system preventing ice on wings and tail.}{catAE}
\dictentry{Indicated Airspeed}{Speed read directly from the airspeed indicator.}{catAE}

\dictentry{X Band}{A frequency band around 8-12 GHz for high data rate.}{catSS}
\dictentry{Yarkovsky Effect}{A force on rotating asteroids from thermal emission.}{catSS}
\dictentry{Yo-Yo Deploy}{A technique for reducing spin before separation.}{catSS}
\end{multicols}
